                $(document).on("click", ".kooc-page-btn", function(e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    
                    var page = $(this).data("page");
                    var $button = $(this);
                    
                    $(".kooc-page-btn").removeClass("active");
                    $button.addClass("active");
                    
                    $("#kooc-articles").html("<p style=\"text-align: center; padding: 40px;\">Loading...</p>");
                    
                    $.ajax({
                        url: "https://blockonomi.com/wp-admin/admin-ajax.php",
                        type: "POST",
                        data: {
                            action: "kooc_load_page",
                            page: page,
                            nonce: "b9680d9754"
                        },
                        success: function(response) {
                            $(".kooc-aggregator").html(response);
                        },
                        error: function() {
                            $("#kooc-articles").html("<p style=\"text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: red;\">Error loading articles. Please try again.</p>");
                        }
                    });
                });

  <div style="position: relative; width: 100%; height: 100%;">
    <iframe 
      id="knockoutstocks-iframe"
      style="position: absolute; top: 50%; left: 50%; transform: translate(-50%, -50%); width: 90%; max-width: 900px; height: 90%; max-height: 600px; border: none; border-radius: 12px;"

<script>
(function() {
  // Wait for DOM to be fully loaded
  function initModal() {
    // Check if user has already closed the modal
    if (localStorage.getItem('knockoutstocks-modal-closed') === 'true') {
      return; // Don't show modal if previously closed
    }

    var modal = document.getElementById('knockoutstocks-modal');
    var iframe = document.getElementById('knockoutstocks-iframe');
    
    if (!modal || !iframe) {
      console.error('KnockoutStocks: Modal elements not found');
      return;
    }
    
    var modalShown = false;

    // Show modal after 5 seconds
    setTimeout(function() {
      if (!modalShown && !localStorage.getItem('knockoutstocks-modal-closed')) {
        // Load iframe source only when showing (faster initial page load)
        iframe.src = 'https://knockoutstocks.com/embed/signup';
        modal.style.display = 'block';
        // Fade in animation
        setTimeout(function() {
          modal.style.opacity = '1';
        }, 10);
        modalShown = true;
      }
    }, 0);

    // Close modal and remember user's choice
    function closeModal() {
      modal.style.opacity = '0';
      setTimeout(function() {
        modal.style.display = 'none';
      }, 300);
      localStorage.setItem('knockoutstocks-modal-closed', 'true');
      modalShown = false;
    }

    // Close when clicking outside
    modal.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      if (e.target === modal) {
        closeModal();
      }
    });

    // Listen for messages from iframe
    window.addEventListener('message', function(e) {
      if (e.data.type === 'knockoutstocks-close-modal' || e.data.type === 'knockoutstocks-signup-success') {
        closeModal();
      }
    });

    // Close on ESC key
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && modal.style.display === 'block') {
        closeModal();
      }
    });
  }

  // Initialize when DOM is ready
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initModal);
  } else {
    initModal();
  }
})();
</script></div>

<h2><span class="ez-toc-section" id="Guide_to_Crypto_Gambling_With_Base"></span>Guide to Crypto Gambling With Base<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h2>
<p>Crypto casinos have emerged as a dominant force in the online gambling industry over the past decade. Base, as Coinbase&#8217;s Layer 2 blockchain solution, represents the cutting edge of accessible crypto gambling with its combination of low fees and mainstream integration.</p>
<p>The popularity of using Base for online gambling continues to surge as players discover the benefits of Layer 2 technology. High Ethereum gas fees and slow transaction times have driven many gamblers toward Base solutions that offer near-instant transactions at a fraction of the cost.</p>
<figure id="attachment_124112-2" aria-describedby="caption-attachment-124112-2" style="width: 1000px" class="wp-caption aligncenter"><img loading="lazy" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-124112" src="https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/Thrill-homepage.jpg" alt="Thrill Homepage" width="1000" height="700" srcset="https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/Thrill-homepage.jpg 1000w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/Thrill-homepage-300x210.jpg 300w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/Thrill-homepage-768x538.jpg 768w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/Thrill-homepage-50x35.jpg 50w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/Thrill-homepage-150x105.jpg 150w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/08/Thrill-homepage-450x315.jpg 450w" sizes="(max-width: 1000px) 100vw, 1000px" /><figcaption id="caption-attachment-124112-2" class="wp-caption-text"><a href="https://blockonomi.com/out/thrill">Thrill Homepage</a></figcaption></figure>
<h2><span class="ez-toc-section" id="About_Base"></span>About Base<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h2>
<p>Base was launched in August 2023 by Coinbase as a secure, low-cost Ethereum Layer 2 blockchain built on Optimism&#8217;s OP Stack. It inherits Ethereum&#8217;s security while processing transactions off the main chain, resulting in significantly faster speeds and lower fees.</p>
<p>Unlike standalone blockchains that must establish their own security, Base leverages Ethereum&#8217;s proven infrastructure while solving its scalability challenges. This approach makes it particularly attractive for online gambling, where transaction costs and speed directly impact the player experience.</p>
<p>Base&#8217;s integration with Coinbase provides seamless onboarding for millions of existing cryptocurrency users. This connection to one of the world&#8217;s largest and most trusted crypto exchanges gives Base immediate credibility and accessibility that newer chains struggle to achieve.</p>
<figure id="attachment_123270" aria-describedby="caption-attachment-123270" style="width: 1000px" class="wp-caption aligncenter"><img loading="lazy" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-123270" src="https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/jackbit-homepage.gif" alt="Jackbit Homepage" width="1000" height="700" /><figcaption id="caption-attachment-123270" class="wp-caption-text"><a href="https://blockonomi.com/out/jackbit">Jackbit Homepage</a></figcaption></figure>
<h2><span class="ez-toc-section" id="Understanding_Crypto_Casinos"></span>Understanding Crypto Casinos<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h2>
<p>Crypto casinos are online gambling platforms that accept cryptocurrencies as their primary or exclusive payment method. They operate similarly to traditional online casinos but leverage blockchain technology to offer enhanced features like provably fair gaming and instant withdrawals.</p>
<p>These platforms differ fundamentally from traditional online casinos in how they handle transactions and verify game fairness. Instead of relying on third-party payment processors and auditing firms, crypto casinos use blockchain technology to provide transparent, verifiable gaming experiences.</p>
<p>The advantages of using cryptocurrencies for gambling extend beyond simple convenience. Players enjoy enhanced privacy since crypto transactions don&#8217;t require personal banking information, reducing identity theft risks and maintaining confidentiality.</p>
<p>Transaction speed represents another major advantage, with Base deposits typically confirming within seconds and withdrawals processing almost instantly. This efficiency eliminates the frustration of waiting for traditional banking systems to process gambling winnings.</p>
<p>Lower fees make cryptocurrency gambling more economical for players. Traditional casinos often charge substantial processing fees for deposits and withdrawals, while Base transactions typically cost pennies or less, making it ideal for players of all stake levels.</p>
<p>Global accessibility allows players from jurisdictions with restrictive banking regulations to participate freely. Base transcends geographical boundaries, enabling gambling access regardless of local banking policies or currency restrictions.</p>
<figure id="attachment_100541" aria-describedby="caption-attachment-100541" style="width: 1000px" class="wp-caption aligncenter"><img loading="lazy" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-100541" src="https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/BC-Game-Homepage.jpg" alt="BC Game Homepage" width="1000" height="700" srcset="https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/BC-Game-Homepage.jpg 1000w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/BC-Game-Homepage-300x210.jpg 300w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/BC-Game-Homepage-768x538.jpg 768w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/BC-Game-Homepage-50x35.jpg 50w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/BC-Game-Homepage-150x105.jpg 150w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/BC-Game-Homepage-450x315.jpg 450w" sizes="(max-width: 1000px) 100vw, 1000px" /><figcaption id="caption-attachment-100541" class="wp-caption-text"><a href="https://blockonomi.com/out/bc-game">BC Game Homepage</a></figcaption></figure>
<h2><span class="ez-toc-section" id="Legal_Status_of_Crypto_Casinos"></span>Legal Status of Crypto Casinos<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h2>
<p>The legal landscape for crypto casinos varies significantly across different jurisdictions and continues to evolve. Many countries haven&#8217;t established specific regulations for cryptocurrency gambling, creating a gray area where these platforms operate without explicit legal approval or prohibition.</p>
<p>Some jurisdictions like Curacao, Malta, and Gibraltar have embraced crypto casinos by offering specific licensing frameworks. These licenses provide legitimacy and regulatory oversight while allowing casinos to serve international markets with cryptocurrencies.</p>
<p>Players should understand that crypto casino legality depends on their location and local gambling laws. While Base transactions themselves may be legal, using them for gambling might violate local regulations depending on jurisdiction.</p>
<p>Licensing requirements for crypto casinos typically include operational standards, security protocols, and financial audits. Reputable casinos display their licensing information prominently, usually from recognized authorities like the Curacao Gaming Control Board or the Malta Gaming Authority.</p>
<p>The regulatory environment continues to mature as governments recognize cryptocurrency&#8217;s growing role in online gambling. This evolution suggests more structured frameworks will emerge, potentially providing greater player protection and industry legitimacy.</p>

<h2><span class="ez-toc-section" id="How_We_Selected_the_Best_Base_Casinos"></span>How We Selected the Best Base Casinos<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h2>
<p>Our selection process involves rigorous testing across multiple criteria to ensure we recommend only trustworthy, high-quality Base casinos. We create accounts, make deposits, play games, and process withdrawals to experience exactly what regular players encounter.</p>
<p>Security measures receive our primary attention since protecting user funds and data is paramount. We verify SSL encryption, examine wallet security protocols, and assess each casino&#8217;s track record for maintaining player fund safety.</p>
<p>Game selection quality and variety factor heavily into our evaluations. We assess the range of slots, table games, live dealer options, and specialty games while testing gameplay smoothness and graphics quality.</p>
<p>Bonus offers are scrutinized for fairness and attainability. We analyze wagering requirements, game restrictions, and terms to determine whether promotions provide genuine value or simply serve as marketing gimmicks.</p>
<p>Customer support responsiveness is tested through multiple channels at various times. We pose questions via live chat, email, and support tickets to gauge knowledge, helpfulness, and resolution speed.</p>
<p>Withdrawal processing speed and reliability are confirmed through actual transactions. We monitor how quickly casinos process requests and whether they honor their stated withdrawal policies without unnecessary complications.</p>

<h2><span class="ez-toc-section" id="Features_to_Look_for_in_a_Crypto_Casino"></span>Features to Look for in a Crypto Casino<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>Game Variety</strong>: Look for casinos offering hundreds or thousands of games including slots, blackjack, roulette, baccarat, poker variants, live dealer games, and specialty options like crash games and dice</li>
<li><strong>Security Measures</strong>: Prioritize platforms with SSL encryption, two-factor authentication, cold wallet storage for funds, regular security audits, and a proven track record of protecting player assets</li>
<li><strong>Bonuses and Promotions</strong>: Evaluate welcome bonuses, reload bonuses, cashback offers, VIP programs, and wagering requirements to ensure they&#8217;re reasonable and achievable</li>
<li><strong>User Interface and Experience</strong>: Choose casinos with intuitive navigation, mobile optimization, fast loading times, smooth gameplay, and clean design that enhances rather than hinders your experience</li>
<li><strong>Customer Support</strong>: Ensure 24/7 availability through live chat, email support, comprehensive FAQ sections, and knowledgeable representatives who can resolve issues efficiently</li>
<li><strong>Provably Fair</strong>: Select casinos offering provably fair games that allow you to verify each result&#8217;s randomness through cryptographic algorithms, ensuring complete transparency and fairness</li>
</ul>
<h2><span class="ez-toc-section" id="How_to_Get_Started_with_Base_Gambling"></span>How to Get Started with Base Gambling<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h2>
<p>Setting up a crypto wallet represents your first step toward Base gambling. You can choose between software wallets like MetaMask, Coinbase Wallet, or Rainbow Wallet, each offering different features while supporting Base network natively.</p>
<p>MetaMask is the most widely used option and requires adding Base as a custom network. Coinbase Wallet offers seamless Base integration and connects directly to your Coinbase account if you have one, simplifying the onboarding process significantly.</p>
<p>Purchasing cryptocurrency for use on Base typically starts with acquiring ETH or USDC on the Ethereum mainnet. You can buy these through exchanges like Coinbase, Kraken, or Binance by creating an account, verifying your identity, and linking a payment method.</p>
<p>Bridging your assets to Base is necessary since most exchanges don&#8217;t support direct Base deposits yet. The official Base bridge allows you to transfer ETH or USDC from Ethereum mainnet to Base, typically completing within minutes at minimal cost.</p>
<p>Alternative bridging options include third-party bridges like Hop Protocol or Synapse, which may offer competitive fees and support additional tokens. Always use established, audited bridges to ensure your funds remain secure during the transfer process.</p>
<p>Making deposits to Base casinos involves copying your unique casino wallet address and ensuring your wallet is connected to the Base network. Always verify you&#8217;re on the correct network before sending, as sending from the wrong chain can result in lost funds.</p>
<p>Most deposits confirm within seconds on Base due to the network&#8217;s fast block times and low congestion. Some casinos credit deposits instantly, while others may require one or two confirmations before funds become available for play.</p>
<p>Withdrawals follow a similar process but in reverse—you provide your personal wallet address and ensure it&#8217;s configured for the Base network. Processing times vary by casino but typically complete within minutes to a few hours, offering significant speed advantages over traditional payment methods.</p>
<figure id="attachment_100952-2" aria-describedby="caption-attachment-100952-2" style="width: 1000px" class="wp-caption aligncenter"><img loading="lazy" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-100952" src="https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/Rakebit-Homepage.jpg" alt="Rakebit Homepage" width="1000" height="700" srcset="https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/Rakebit-Homepage.jpg 1000w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/Rakebit-Homepage-300x210.jpg 300w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/Rakebit-Homepage-768x538.jpg 768w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/Rakebit-Homepage-50x35.jpg 50w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/Rakebit-Homepage-150x105.jpg 150w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/08/Rakebit-Homepage-450x315.jpg 450w" sizes="(max-width: 1000px) 100vw, 1000px" /><figcaption id="caption-attachment-100952-2" class="wp-caption-text"><a href="https://blockonomi.com/out/rakebit">Rakebit Homepage</a></figcaption></figure>
<h2><span class="ez-toc-section" id="Responsible_Gambling_in_Base_Casinos"></span>Responsible Gambling in Base Casinos<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h2>
<p>Setting limits protects you from the rapid pace and accessibility that make crypto gambling potentially risky. Establish deposit limits, loss limits, and session time limits before you begin playing, treating these boundaries as non-negotiable rules.</p>
<p>Many Base casinos offer built-in responsible gambling tools allowing you to set these limits directly in your account. Utilize these features to create automatic safeguards that prevent excessive gambling even in moments of poor judgment.</p>
<p>Self-exclusion options provide a powerful tool if you feel gambling is becoming problematic. These programs allow you to voluntarily ban yourself from a casino for predetermined periods, ranging from days to permanent exclusion.</p>
<p>Recognizing problem gambling signs early is crucial for maintaining healthy gambling habits. Warning signs include chasing losses, gambling with money you can&#8217;t afford to lose, neglecting responsibilities, and feeling anxious or irritable when not gambling.</p>
<p>Resources for problem gambling are available through organizations like Gamblers Anonymous, the National Council on Problem Gambling, and GamCare. These organizations offer counseling, support groups, and helplines staffed by professionals who understand gambling addiction.</p>
<figure id="attachment_94792-2" aria-describedby="caption-attachment-94792-2" style="width: 1000px" class="wp-caption aligncenter"><img loading="lazy" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-94792" src="https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/Metawin-Homepage.jpg" alt="Metawin Homepage" width="1000" height="700" srcset="https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/Metawin-Homepage.jpg 1000w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/Metawin-Homepage-300x210.jpg 300w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/Metawin-Homepage-768x538.jpg 768w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/Metawin-Homepage-50x35.jpg 50w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/Metawin-Homepage-150x105.jpg 150w, https://blockonomi.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/Metawin-Homepage-450x315.jpg 450w" sizes="(max-width: 1000px) 100vw, 1000px" /><figcaption id="caption-attachment-94792-2" class="wp-caption-text"><a href="https://blockonomi.com/out/metawin">Metawin Homepage</a></figcaption></figure>
<h2><span class="ez-toc-section" id="Conclusion"></span>Conclusion<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h2>
<p>We&#8217;ve thoroughly tested and evaluated the Base casinos on our list to ensure they meet the highest standards of security, fairness, and player satisfaction. Each platform offers unique strengths, but all provide reliable, entertaining gambling experiences with the advantages that Base&#8217;s Layer 2 technology offers.</p>
<p>Whether you prioritize game variety, generous bonuses, or lightning-fast withdrawals with minimal fees, you&#8217;ll find trustworthy options among our recommendations. You can confidently choose any casino from our list knowing it has passed our rigorous testing process.</p>
<p>Best of luck at the tables!</p>
<h2><span class="ez-toc-section" id="Pros_and_Cons_of_Base_Casinos"></span>Pros and Cons of Base Casinos<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h2>
<p><strong>Advantages</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Extremely Low Fees</strong>: Transaction costs are typically pennies or less, making Base ideal for players of all stake levels including micro-betting</li>
<li><strong>Lightning-Fast Transactions</strong>: Deposits confirm in seconds and withdrawals process almost instantly thanks to Base&#8217;s Layer 2 architecture</li>
<li><strong>Ethereum Security</strong>: Benefits from Ethereum&#8217;s proven security model while avoiding its high costs and congestion issues</li>
<li><strong>Easy Coinbase Integration</strong>: Seamless connection with Coinbase accounts provides simple onboarding for millions of existing crypto users</li>
<li><strong>Enhanced Privacy</strong>: Transactions don&#8217;t require personal banking details, maintaining anonymity and reducing identity theft risks</li>
<li><strong>Provably Fair Gaming</strong>: Blockchain technology enables transparent verification of game outcomes for complete fairness assurance</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Disadvantages</strong></p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Limited Casino Adoption</strong>: Fewer casinos accept Base compared to more established cryptocurrencies, though adoption is growing rapidly</li>
<li><strong>Bridging Requirements</strong>: Most users need to bridge assets from Ethereum mainnet, adding an extra step compared to direct deposits</li>
<li><strong>Network Maturity</strong>: As a newer blockchain launched in 2023, Base has less operational history than established networks</li>
<li><strong>Price Volatility</strong>: Crypto assets on Base fluctuate in value, meaning your balance can decrease in fiat terms even without gambling</li>
<li><strong>Learning Curve</strong>: Understanding Layer 2 networks, bridging, and wallet configuration requires education that may intimidate newcomers</li>
<li><strong>Regulatory Uncertainty</strong>: Unclear legal status in many jurisdictions creates potential risks regarding legality and player protection</li>
</ul>
<h2><span class="ez-toc-section" id="Frequently_Asked_Questions"></span>Frequently Asked Questions<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h2>
<h3><span class="ez-toc-section" id="Is_Base_gambling_legal"></span>Is Base gambling legal?<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h3>
<p>Base gambling legality depends entirely on your jurisdiction&#8217;s laws regarding online gambling and cryptocurrency use. While Base transactions themselves are legal in most countries, using them for gambling may violate local regulations. Research your local laws or consult legal counsel before participating in crypto gambling to ensure compliance.</p>
<h3><span class="ez-toc-section" id="How_long_do_Base_casino_transactions_take"></span>How long do Base casino transactions take?<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h3>
<p>Base deposits typically confirm within seconds thanks to the network&#8217;s fast block times and Layer 2 architecture. Withdrawals from reputable casinos usually process within minutes to a few hours. This represents a dramatic speed advantage over both traditional payment methods and even many other blockchain networks.</p>
<h3><span class="ez-toc-section" id="How_do_I_get_cryptocurrency_onto_Base"></span>How do I get cryptocurrency onto Base?<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h3>
<p>You&#8217;ll first need to purchase ETH or USDC on an exchange like Coinbase, then bridge these assets to the Base network using the official Base bridge or alternatives like Hop Protocol. The bridging process typically takes a few minutes and costs minimal fees, after which your funds are ready for use at Base casinos.</p>
<h3><span class="ez-toc-section" id="Are_Base_casinos_safe"></span>Are Base casinos safe?<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h3>
<p>Reputable Base casinos with proper licensing, SSL encryption, and proven track records are generally safe. Base itself benefits from Ethereum&#8217;s security while being backed by Coinbase&#8217;s reputation. However, research each casino individually, as the cryptocurrency space attracts some unscrupulous operators regardless of the underlying blockchain technology.</p>
<h3><span class="ez-toc-section" id="What_is_provably_fair_gaming"></span>What is provably fair gaming?<span class="ez-toc-section-end"></span></h3>
<p>Provably fair is a technology unique to crypto casinos that allows you to verify each game outcome&#8217;s randomness and fairness. Using cryptographic algorithms, you can independently confirm that results weren&#8217;t manipulated by the casino. This transparency is impossible with traditional online casinos and represents a major advantage of blockchain-based gambling on networks like Base.</p>

<script>
(function() {
  // Wait for DOM to be fully loaded
  function initModal() {
    // Check if user has already closed the modal
    if (localStorage.getItem('knockoutstocks-modal-closed') === 'true') {
      return; // Don't show modal if previously closed
    }

    var modal = document.getElementById('knockoutstocks-modal');
    var iframe = document.getElementById('knockoutstocks-iframe');
    
    if (!modal || !iframe) {
      console.error('KnockoutStocks: Modal elements not found');
      return;
    }
    
    var modalShown = false;

    // Show modal after 5 seconds
    setTimeout(function() {
      if (!modalShown && !localStorage.getItem('knockoutstocks-modal-closed')) {
        // Load iframe source only when showing (faster initial page load)
        iframe.src = 'https://knockoutstocks.com/embed/signup';
        modal.style.display = 'block';
        // Fade in animation
        setTimeout(function() {
          modal.style.opacity = '1';
        }, 10);
        modalShown = true;
      }
    }, 0);

    // Close modal and remember user's choice
    function closeModal() {
      modal.style.opacity = '0';
      setTimeout(function() {
        modal.style.display = 'none';
      }, 300);
      localStorage.setItem('knockoutstocks-modal-closed', 'true');
      modalShown = false;
    }

    // Close when clicking outside
    modal.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      if (e.target === modal) {
        closeModal();
      }
    });

    // Listen for messages from iframe
    window.addEventListener('message', function(e) {
      if (e.data.type === 'knockoutstocks-close-modal' || e.data.type === 'knockoutstocks-signup-success') {
        closeModal();
      }
    });

    // Close on ESC key
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && modal.style.display === 'block') {
        closeModal();
      }
    });
  }

  // Initialize when DOM is ready
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initModal);
  } else {
    initModal();
  }
})();
</script></div>
